# U3 number ledger (2026-11-17)

Every quantitative token in the in-scope files (13 appendices, 3 analysis bundles, the gap analysis), outside code blocks and front matter, one row per distinct value per line, after this pass's edits. Generated by an extraction script and then classified, so a row is a number *as it appears*, and line numbers are those of the edited files on 2026-11-17.

Verdict vocabulary follows U1 where a check was made at a primary (`verified-at-primary`, `not-in-source`, `unverifiable-no-primary`, `dead-link`), and adds four honest classes for rows this pass did not re-check one by one:

- `withdrawn ...` rows sit inside a strikethrough or carry a dated withdrawal note at the point of use, so they are the record, not live claims.
- `author-modelled / illustrative` rows are labelled in the text as a model, worksheet, scenario or example.
- `traced-to-public-lab` rows name a first-party measurement in the public lab repository; the figures this pass re-derived are marked `verified-at-primary` instead.
- `cited` and `named-primary-url` rows name a bibliography tier, footnote or URL at the point of use that this pass did not re-fetch.

`unverifiable-no-primary` means the line states a number without naming any source, which is the backlog for the next sweep; it does not mean the number is wrong.
